On August 4, 2026, American Public University System combined with Rasmussen University and Hondros College of Nursing to form a single higher education institution. American Public University System (the System) now includes American Public University, American Military University, Rasmussen University, and Hondros College of Nursing. The System is accredited by the Higher Learning Commission and is a wholly owned subsidiary of American Public Education, Inc. (APEI). We are continuing to update our website to reflect our new structure.

Undergraduate
Academic
Military Service & Education
Traditional
air university associate to baccalaureate cooperative (au-abc)
Recommended Transfer Credit
60 semester hours (SH) guaranteed applied to the following programs:
BA in Criminal Justice
BA in Homeland Security
BA in Intelligence Studies
BA in Management
BA in Political Science
BA in Reverse Logistics Management
BA in Security Management
BAS in Technical Management
BS in Sports Management
Eligibility
To be eligible for participation in this program, students must meet the following criteria:
Have a conferred CCAF AAS degree.
Be enrolled and admitted in one of the approved APUS degree programs listed above. Note: Only the programs in the list above are eligible for the AU-ABC program transfer credit.
New students will be admitted into the current catalog APUS degree program.
Airmen and/or Guardians with a conferred CCAF degree who participate in the AU-ABC Program are guaranteed to complete their bachelor's degree in 60 semester hours or less with APUS.
A maximum of 60 semester hours will remain for completion of the selected degree program.
Up to 15 semester hours will be applied to the General Education area.
Credit from the official CCAF transcript may come from multiple sources including academic credit from other institutions or credit by examination, and will be applied according to current APUS transfer credit policies.
All coursework transferred into the CCAF AAS degree is part of the total credits applied toward a student's APUS bachelor's degree program; this includes any credit that was transferred from APUS to CCAF for completion of the AAS degree.
Students wishing to participate in the AU-ABC Program may NOT add a minor or certificate to their selected APUS degree program. The addition of a minor or certificate prevents APUS from being able to meet the terms of the AU-ABC Co-operative Agreement as set by CCAF, thus making any student who chooses to add either or both of those elements to their chosen degree program ineligible to participate in the AU-ABC Program.
Program Changes
Students may change from one approved AU-ABC degree to another, or have the option of changing their program of study in order to take advantage of the AU-ABC Program; however, this should only be done AFTER a student has consulted his/her APUS Student Advisor as it could impact their previous TCE award. For questions about changing an APUS Academic Program, please contact advising@apus.edu .
If a student is looking to change from an existing approved AU-ABC degree to a degree that is not part of the AU-ABC program, they may not receive the same courses that were awarded in their previous TCE. Any student initiating a program change voluntarily agrees to:
all new program requirements for their new APUS program as outlined in the current APUS catalog,
forfeiting their previous TCE award, and
having a new TCE performed which will be conducted using current transfer credit policies and guidelines.
APUS courses start the first Monday of each month and are 8-weeks in length. Please see the current course schedule for specifics.
If you need help or have questions, our transfer credit staff is available and will guide you through the process step-by-step.

Eligibility
Students who submit certificates of completion for ATS 107, ATS 108, ATS 109, ATS 110 or ATS 111 WILL NOT receive duplicate credit if they also submit ATS 113
Students who submit certificates of completion for ATS 107, ATS 109 or ATS 110 WILL NOT receive duplicate credit if they also submit ATS 114
For submitted certificates to be eligible they MUST display the Amentum or A-T Solutions, Inc. course number and indicate the course completion date was AFTER the effective date listed.
Please include a copy of your certificate when you submit the transfer credit evaluation form .
Undergraduate
Academic
Traditional
american nurses credentialing center (ancc)
Recommended Transfer Credit
The maximum transfer credit award possible is 3 semester hours (SH) based on the following:
Registered Nurse Residency or Registered Nurse Fellowship
Effective Dates: August 12, 2021 – Present
NURS300 – Transitions in Nursing 3 SH
Individual transfer credit award will vary based on the requirements of your selected degree program.
Note: The credit recommendations on this page are reviewed each catalog year and are subject to change.
Eligibility
Any hospital or medical center offering a nurse residency or nurse fellowship program accredited by ANCC is eligible for this policy. Accreditation can be confirmed by locating the ANCC logo or seal on the certificate or verified at https://www.nursingworld.org/organizational-programs/accreditation/find-an-accredited-organization
Please include a copy of your certificate of completion when you submit the transfer credit evaluation form .
You may pursue any undergraduate degree program, but you are most likely to maximize your transfer credit award if you are pursuing the Bachelor of Science in Nursing .

Individual transfer credit award will vary based on the requirements of your selected degree program. Credits awarded are considered nontraditional transfer credit.
Note: The credit recommendations on this page are reviewed each catalog year and are subject to change.
Eligibility
The training program must be for "basic" training; specialized law enforcement programs do not qualify for this internal policy.
The training program must have been completed on or after January 1, 1990.
The training program must incorporate a minimum of 300 contact hours for completion.
NOTE: Programs for corrections, detention, reserve officers, radar, taser, or other specialized trainings DO NOT qualify for this internal policy and are NOT acceptable for transfer credit review.
Students may only receive transfer credit for ONE BLET program or Peace Officer Standards and Training (POST) course. Any additional BLET or POST course is considered duplicate – even if completed through a different organization or in a different state – and will NOT be awarded any additional transfer credit.
Please include a copy of your certificate when you submit the transfer credit evaluation form .
The training program must be for law enforcement, police officer, or peace officer training only.
You may pursue any undergraduate degree program, but you are most likely to maximize your transfer credit award if you are pursuing a Bachelor of Arts in Criminal Justice with a General Concentration.

*Omnibus
credit applies to a student's specific area of study within their
Concentration requirements when the university does not offer an
equivalent course. This credit may be treated as Elective credit
depending on the specific program requirements.
Individual
transfer credit award will vary based on the requirements of your
selected degree program.
Credits awarded are considered traditional
transfer credit.
Note: The credit recommendations on this page are reviewed each catalog year and are subject to change.
Eligibility
Credit can only be awarded for coursework that appears in Section I: DAU Classroom and Web Course Completions.
Your official Defense Acquisition University transcript will be required after you submit the transfer credit evaluation form .

*Omnibus credit applies to a student's specific area of study within their Concentration requirements when the university does not offer an equivalent course. This credit may be treated as Elective credit depending on the specific program requirements.
Individual transfer credit award will vary based on the requirements of your selected degree program. Credits awarded are considered traditional transfer credit.
Note: The credit recommendations on this page are reviewed each catalog year and are subject to change.
Eligibility
Credit will be awarded for the highest level attained for each certification area. Additional lower certifications within the same certification area are considered duplicative and will not be awarded.
Defense Acquisition University has replaced their previous format of certification level (I, II, III, etc.) with functional areas (Practitioner, Advanced, etc.). If both formats are submitted for the same certification area, the most advantageous credit recommendation will be awarded; the other is considered duplicative and will not be awarded.
Please note: select Defense Acquisition University course completions may be considered duplicative of certifications in this policy. For more information, please contact us at creditaward@apus.edu .
Please include a copy of your certificate when you submit the transfer credit evaluation form .

their Concentration requirements when the university does not offer an

depending on the specific program requirements.

Undergraduate
Non-Traditional
Public Safety Training
federal emergency management agency (fema) coursework - emergency management institute (emi)
Recommended Transfer Credit
The maximum transfer credit award possible varies based on the following. Contact Transfer Credit Award for more information.
American Council on Education (ACE) reviewed FEMA coursework.
The University will follow the
recommendations made by ACE for any FEMA coursework that ACE has
reviewed. If the ACE recommendation for the course completed is for
credit at the baccalaureate (lower or upper) level, the University will
accept that number of semester hours for the course. Note that
Vocational credit recommendations are not acceptable for credit at the
postsecondary education level.
To search for and view applicable ACE credit recommendations, see the National Emergency Training Center (EMI) tab of the ACE National Guide .
FEMA coursework not reviewed by ACE:
Any remaining coursework listed on a
submitted FEMA transcript that has not been reviewed by ACE will be
converted from Continuing Education Units (CEUs) to Semester Hours (SH).
The University will accept a maximum of up to 12 SH of transfer credit
from non-ACE reviewed FEMA coursework, which can only be applied to the
Elective requirements of an undergraduate degree program.
CEUs are converted to SH using the
equation: CEUs/1.5=SH; any decimal credit is reduced to the nearest
whole number. For example, 6.3 CEUs, divided by 1.5, equals 4.2.
Therefore, in this example, the student would be eligible for a maximum
of up to 4 SH of Elective transfer credit.
Frederick Community College coursework:
If a student has successfully
completed associated coursework at Frederick Community College (FCC)
complimenting previously completed FEMA coursework, their FCC coursework
can be reviewed for potential traditional academic transfer credit.
Students must submit an official Frederick Community College transcript to be eligible for potential academic transfer credit.
Individual transfer credit award will
vary based on the requirements of your selected degree program. Credits
awarded are considered nontraditional transfer credit.
Note: The credit recommendations on this page are reviewed each catalog year and are subject to change.
Eligibility
Please include a copy of your FEMA official transcript or Civil Air Patrol (CAP) transcript when you submit the transfer credit evaluation form .
You may pursue any undergraduate degree program.

Eligibility
For CAP and/or SSCP, a student must have been certified or re-certified within 5 years of the date of their TCE. To qualify for this internal policy as a CISSP, a student must have a valid certification at the time their TCE is completed. For example, a student with a CISSP certification expiring in December of 2018 who requests their Initial TCE in July 2018 would qualify; however, if that same student were to request a Program Change in 2019, the existing certification on file would no longer be considered valid, and transfer credit would not be awarded without a new, valid CISSP certification being submitted.
Please include a copy of your certification(s) from (ISC) 2 when you submit the transfer credit evaluation form .
You may pursue any undergraduate degree program at the university, but you are most likely to maximize your transfer credit award if you are pursuing one of these degrees:
Bachelor of Science in Computer Technology
Bachelor of Science in Information Technology (general concentration)
Bachelorof Science in Information Technology Management

*Omnibus credit applies to a student's specific area of study within their Concentration requirements when the university does not offer an equivalent course. This credit may be treated as Elective credit depending on the specific program requirements.
Note: The credit recommendations on this page are reviewed each catalog year and are subject to change.
Eligibility
Please include a copy of your certificate when you submit the transfer credit evaluation form .
You may pursue any undergraduate degree program, but you are most likely to maximize your transfer credit award if you are pursuing a Bachelor of Arts in Intelligence Studies .

Credit cannot be combined for each level completed; only the higher amount of transfer credit will be awarded when multiple certifications within the training hierarchy are submitted. Credits awarded are considered nontraditional transfer credit.
Note: The credit recommendations on this page are reviewed each catalog year and are subject to change.
Eligibility
Credit will be awarded for the highest certification attained. Additional lower certifications are considered duplicative and will not be awarded.
Please include a copy of your certificate when you submit the transfer credit evaluation form .

Note: The credit recommendations on this page are reviewed each catalog year and are subject to change.
Eligibility
While Newstate University offers micro-courses, please note that these are not acceptable for credit at this time. Micro-course codes have an “M” followed by a number attached to the course number. For example, PM-100 M1 , wherein M1 indicates this is the first mini-course in a series. In this example, only the full PM100 course would be acceptable for transfer.
Please have your official Newstate University transcript sent to APUS when you submit the transfer credit evaluation form .

Individual transfer credit award will vary based on the requirements of your selected degree program. Credits awarded are considered nontraditional transfer credit.
Note: The credit recommendations on this page are reviewed each catalog year and are subject to change.
Eligibility
The training program must be for "basic" training; specialized law enforcement programs do not qualify for this internal policy.
The training program must have been completed on or after January 1, 1990.
The training program must incorporate a minimum of 300 contact hours for completion.
NOTE: Programs for corrections, detention, reserve officers, radar, taser, or other specialized trainings DO NOT qualify for this internal policy and are NOT acceptable for transfer credit review.
Students may only receive transfer credit for ONE POST or Basic Law Enforcement Training (BLET). Any additional BLET or POST course is considered duplicate – even if completed through a different organization or in a different state – and will NOT be awarded any additional transfer credit.
Please include a copy of your certificate when you submit the transfer credit evaluation form .
The training program must be for law enforcement, police officer, or peace officer training only.
You may pursue any undergraduate degree program, but you are most likely to maximize your transfer credit award if you are pursuing a Bachelor of Arts in Criminal Justice with a General Concentration.
Undergraduate
Professional Training Courses & Certifications
Non-Traditional
project management institute (pmi)
Recommended Transfer Credit
The maximum transfer credit award possible is 12 semester hours (SH) based on the following:
Project Management Professional Certification (PMP)
BUSN333 – Project Management for Business Analysts
3 SH Undergraduate-Level Credit in Elective
BUSN451 – Introduction to Business Project Management
3 SH Undergraduate-Level Credit in Elective
BUSN452 – Project Schedule and Scope Management OR
ITMG221 – IT Project Management
3 SH Undergraduate-Level Credit in Elective
BUSN453 – Project Integration, Quality, Resource & Stakeholder Management OR
ITMG223 / ITMG222 – IT Project Management Using Microsoft Project
3 SH Undergraduate-Level Credit in Elective
Individual transfer credit award will vary based on the requirements of your selected degree program. Credits awarded are considered nontraditional transfer credit.
Note: The credit recommendations on this page are reviewed each catalog year and are subject to change.
Eligibility
PMP certification is only valid up until the expiration date indicated on the certificate.
To qualify a student must have a valid certification at the time their TCE is completed. For example, a student with a PMP certification expiring in December of 2012 who requests their Initial TCE in July 2012 would qualify; however, if that same student was to request a Program Change in 2013, the existing certification on file would no longer be considered valid, and transfer credit would not be awarded without a new, valid PMP certification being submitted.
Please include a copy of your certificate when you submit the transfer credit evaluation form .
You may pursue any undergraduate degree program but you are most likely to maximize your transfer credit award if you are pursuing a Bachelor of Business Administration with a General Concentration

Individual transfer credit award will vary based on the requirements of your selected degree program. Credits awarded are considered nontraditional transfer credit.
Note: The credit recommendations on this page are reviewed each catalog year and are subject to change.
Eligibility
To receive transfer credit, the Saylor Direct Credit proctored exam must be passed with a score of 70% or higher.
Please provide your ACE via Credly or your official Saylor transcript when you submit the transfer credit evaluation form . Course completion certificates are not acceptable for transfer.

Please include a copy of your Sophia transcript when you submit the transfer credit evaluation form . APUS reserves the right to request an official transcript for verification purposes.

*Students entering these degrees on or after October 1, 2026 must select the General concentration to be eligible for the Transfer Credit Junior Advantage program.
Individual transfer credit award will vary based on the requirements of your selected degree program.
Note: The credit recommendations on this page are reviewed each catalog year and are subject to change. New students will be admitted into the current catalog APUS degree program.
Eligibility
To be eligible for participation in this program, students must meet the following criteria:
Have a conferred associate or bachelor's degree from an institutionally (nationally or regionally) accredited college or university.
Be enrolled and admitted in one of the approved APUS degree programs listed above. Note: Only the programs in the list above are eligible for the Transfer Credit Junior Advantage program.
Official transcripts for all non-APUS credit sources must be submitted for evaluation.
Students with an eligible conferred degree who participate in the Transfer Credit Junior Advantage program will be able to complete their bachelor's degree in 60 semester hours or less with APUS.
A maximum of 60 semester hours will remain for completion of the selected APUS bachelor's degree program.
Any previously completed APUS coursework will be included in the total credits applied toward a student's APUS bachelor's degree program.
Associates in Applied Sciences (AAS) and all internationally conferred degrees are ineligible.
Adding certain in-degree Certificates and/or Minors to a bachelor's degree may result in ineligibility.
All conferred degrees will be reviewed individually to determine eligibility.
Program Changes
Students may change from one approved Transfer Credit Junior Advantage-eligible degree to another, or have the option of changing their program of study in order to take advantage of the Transfer Credit Junior Advantage program. However, this should only be done AFTER a student has consulted their APUS Student Advisor as it could impact their previous transfer credit award. For questions about changing an APUS Academic Program, please contact advising@apus.edu.
If a student is looking to change from an existing approved Transfer Credit Junior Advantage degree to a degree that is not part of the Transfer Credit Junior Advantage program, they may not receive the same courses that were awarded in their previous transfer credit evaluation. Any student initiating a program change voluntarily agrees to:
All new program requirements for their new APUS program as outlined in the current APUS catalog.
Forfeiting their previous transfer credit award.
Having a new transfer credit evaluation performed, which will be conducted using current transfer credit policies and guidelines.

Individual transfer credit award will vary based on the requirements of your selected degree program.
Transfer credit from this policy is awarded as a nonequivalent block of transfer credit; it is not equivalent to, nor can it satisfy, any specific APUS course requirements except as explicitly indicated as part of the recommended credit (ex. "BIOL201").
For students pursuing the AAS Health Sciences degree program:
All policy credit for the above coursework applies directly to the Major Requirement for this degree. Policy credit exceeding the Major Requirement may be used as elective credit as needed.
Students whose Major Requirements credit award is less than the maximum 28 SH may register for and complete any courses listed within the Major Requirements section of their Academic Plan to fulfill their Major Requirements.
Students are eligible to receive additional transfer credit based on all the documents they submit for evaluation, including direct course matches for courses listed in the Major Requirements area of their degree program, as the block credit for this policy is not directly equivalent to any APUS course.
For students submitting a Joint Services Transcript, associated Military Experience credit awarded for the first rating/skill level (i.e., HN, 68W10, etc.) aligning directly to training within this policy will be considered duplicate credit and will not be awarded. For example, if a student has completed Basic Hospital Corps School (B-300-0010) and receives transfer credit for this policy, they will not receive additional credit for the HN rating ACE recommendations.
For students pursuing any program other than the AAS Health Sciences degree:
All policy credit for the above coursework is acceptable for use toward elective requirements only except as explicitly indicated (ex. "BIOL201").
For students submitting a Joint Services Transcript, associated Military Experience credit awarded for the first rating/skill level (i.e., HN, 68W10, etc.) aligning directly to training within this policy will be considered duplicate credit and will not be awarded. For example, if a student has completed Basic Hospital Corps School (B-300-0010) and receives transfer credit for this policy, they will not receive additional credit for the HN rating ACE recommendations.
Students that receive transfer credit for this policy will not be awarded additional transfer credit for lower-level Emergency Medical Technician training or certification. All National Registry of Emergency Medical Technicians certifications (Basic, Intermediate/Update, Responder, Technician, Advanced Technician, and Paramedic) and lower-level (100-200 level) training and coursework are considered duplicate, regardless of certification date and/or location of military training. Upper-level (300-400 level) training and coursework is not considered duplicative of this policy.
Credits awarded are considered traditional transfer credit.
Note: The credit recommendations on this page are reviewed each catalog year and are subject to change.
Eligibility
Please include an official CCAF Transcript or official Joint Services Transcript when you submit the transfer credit evaluation form .
You may pursue any undergraduate degree program but you are most likely to maximize your transfer credit award if you are pursuing an Associate of Applied Science in Health Sciences .

Eligibility
Army, Navy, and Coast Guard service members must hold recruiter MOS (note: for Army, MOS must reflect Primary designation)
Air Force service members must
be registered for (or have
completed) AAS Human Resource Management or AAS Personnel Management
degree at Community College of the Air Force (CCAF)
have completed the US Air Force Recruiter Course
have a pay grade of E-4 or higher
MOS titles that are NOT eligible for this internal policy:
Army Career Counselor
Army National Guard Recruiting and Retention NCO
Recruit/Assistant Recruit Company Commander
Retention & Transition NCO (RC)
CCAF course credit and Joint
Services Transcript (JST) recommended course and MOS credit associated
with this internal policy will be considered duplicative credit and will
NOT be awarded. For example, if a student holds the 79R MOS and
receives transfer credit from this internal policy, they will NOT
receive any additional credit for the 79R experience ACE recommendation.
Please include JST transcript, CCAF transcript, or other military documents reflecting the Recruiter/Counselor MOS with the transfer credit evaluation form .

*Omnibus credit applies to a student's specific area of study within
their Concentration requirements when the university does not offer an
equivalent course. This credit may be treated as Elective credit
depending on the specific program requirements.
Note: The credit recommendations on this page are reviewed each catalog year and are subject to change.
Eligibility
Please include a copy of your certificate when you submit the transfer credit evaluation form .
You may pursue any graduate degree
program, but you are most likely to maximize your transfer credit award
if you pursue one of the following: Master of Arts in Intelligence Studies with a General, Intelligence Management, or Intelligence Operations concentration.

Mission
Do Not Sell My Personal Information (CA Residents Only)
American Public University System (the System) is accredited by the Higher Learning Commission (HLC) (www.hlcommission.org), an institutional accreditation agency recognized by the U.S. Department of Education. HLC accredits degree-granting institutions nationwide and is also recognized by the Council for Higher Education Accreditation. The System is comprised of American Military University, American Public University, Rasmussen University, and Hondros College of Nursing. The System is authorized by the West Virginia Higher Education Policy Commission and other appropriate agencies in the states where it has campuses. The System is authorized to operate as a postsecondary educational institution by the Illinois Board of Higher Education. American Public University System, Inc., which operates the System, is a wholly owned subsidiary of American Public Education, Inc. American Public University System, American Public University, and American Military University are not affiliated with American University or the U.S. Military.
